### FileConfiguration load Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/configuration/file/FileConfiguration

Methods for loading the FileConfiguration from a file or reader.

```APIDOC
## load(File file)

### Description
Loads this `FileConfiguration` from the specified location. All the values contained within this configuration will be removed, leaving only settings and defaults, and the new values will be loaded from the given file. If the file cannot be loaded for any reason, an exception will be thrown.

### Method
`load`

### Endpoint
N/A (Instance method)

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **file** (File) - Required - File to load from.

### Throws
- `FileNotFoundException` - Thrown when the given file cannot be opened.
- `IOException` - Thrown when the given file cannot be read.
- `InvalidConfigurationException` - Thrown when the given file is not a valid Configuration.
- `IllegalArgumentException` - Thrown when file is null.

### Request Example
```java
FileConfiguration config = new FileConfiguration();
File inputFile = new File("config.yml");
try {
    config.load(inputFile);
} catch (FileNotFoundException e) {
    e.printStackTrace();
} catch (IOException e) {
    e.printStackTrace();
} catch (InvalidConfigurationException e) {
    e.printStackTrace();
}
```

### Response Example
(Method returns void, loads from file.)
```

```APIDOC
## load(Reader reader)

### Description
Loads this `FileConfiguration` from the specified reader. All the values contained within this configuration will be removed, leaving only settings and defaults, and the new values will be loaded from the given stream. 

### Method
`load`

### Endpoint
N/A (Instance method)

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **reader** (Reader) - Required - The reader to load from.

### Throws
- `IOException` - Thrown when underlying reader throws an IOException.
- `InvalidConfigurationException` - Thrown when the reader does not represent a valid Configuration.
- `IllegalArgumentException` - Thrown when reader is null.

### Request Example
```java
FileConfiguration config = new FileConfiguration();
String configData = "key: value\nother: 123";
StringReader reader = new StringReader(configData);
try {
    config.load(reader);
} catch (IOException e) {
    e.printStackTrace();
} catch (InvalidConfigurationException e) {
    e.printStackTrace();
}
```

### Response Example
(Method returns void, loads from reader.)
```

```APIDOC
## load(String file)

### Description
Loads this `FileConfiguration` from the specified location. All the values contained within this configuration will be removed, leaving only settings and defaults, and the new values will be loaded from the given file. If the file cannot be loaded for any reason, an exception will be thrown.

### Method
`load`

### Endpoint
N/A (Instance method)

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **file** (String) - Required - File path to load from.

### Throws
- `FileNotFoundException` - Thrown when the given file cannot be opened.
- `IOException` - Thrown when the given file cannot be read.
- `InvalidConfigurationException` - Thrown when the given file is not a valid Configuration.
- `IllegalArgumentException` - Thrown when file is null.

### Request Example
```java
FileConfiguration config = new FileConfiguration();
try {
    config.load("config.yml");
} catch (FileNotFoundException e) {
    e.printStackTrace();
} catch (IOException e) {
    e.printStackTrace();
} catch (InvalidConfigurationException e) {
    e.printStackTrace();
}
```

### Response Example
(Method returns void, loads from file.)
```

### SpawnerEntry.Equipment API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/block/spawner/class-use/SpawnerEntry.Equipment

Provides methods for getting and setting equipment for spawned entities via SpawnerEntry.

```APIDOC
## SpawnerEntry.Equipment

### Description
This section details the methods related to SpawnerEntry.Equipment within the org.bukkit.block.spawner package. It covers how to retrieve and set the equipment that will be applied to entities spawned by a SpawnerEntry.

### Methods Related to SpawnerEntry.Equipment

#### `getEquipment()`

##### Description
Gets the equipment which will be applied to the spawned entity.

##### Method
`GET`

##### Endpoint
`SpawnerEntry.getEquipment()`

##### Return Type
`@Nullable SpawnerEntry.Equipment`

#### `setEquipment(SpawnerEntry.Equipment equipment)`

##### Description
Sets the equipment which will be applied to the spawned entity.

##### Method
`POST` (or `PUT` depending on context, assuming modification)

##### Endpoint
`SpawnerEntry.setEquipment(equipment: SpawnerEntry.Equipment?)

##### Parameters

*   **Path Parameters**
    *   None

*   **Query Parameters**
    *   None

*   **Request Body**
    *   `equipment` (SpawnerEntry.Equipment) - Required - The equipment to set for the spawned entity.

##### Request Example
```json
{
  "equipment": {
    "itemInMainHand": {
      "type": "DIAMOND_SWORD",
      "amount": 1
    },
    "itemInOffHand": {
      "type": "SHIELD",
      "amount": 1
    }
  }
}
```

##### Response

*   **Success Response (200)**
    *   void

### Constructor with SpawnerEntry.Equipment

#### `SpawnerEntry(EntitySnapshot snapshot, int spawnWeight, SpawnRule spawnRule, SpawnerEntry.Equipment equipment)`

##### Description
Constructs a new SpawnerEntry with the specified entity snapshot, spawn weight, spawn rule, and equipment.

##### Method
`POST`

##### Endpoint
`SpawnerEntry(snapshot: EntitySnapshot, spawnWeight: int, spawnRule: SpawnRule?, equipment: SpawnerEntry.Equipment?)

##### Parameters

*   **Path Parameters**
    *   None

*   **Query Parameters**
    *   None

*   **Request Body**
    *   `snapshot` (EntitySnapshot) - Required - The entity snapshot for the spawner.
    *   `spawnWeight` (int) - Required - The weight of this entry in the spawner.
    *   `spawnRule` (SpawnRule) - Optional - The spawn rule for this entry.
    *   `equipment` (SpawnerEntry.Equipment) - Optional - The equipment to be applied to spawned entities.

##### Request Example
```json
{
  "snapshot": {
    "type": "ZOMBIE",
    "uuid": "..."
  },
  "spawnWeight": 10,
  "spawnRule": {
    "conditions": [],
    "actions": []
  },
  "equipment": {
    "itemInMainHand": {
      "type": "IRON_SWORD",
      "amount": 1
    }
  }
}
```

##### Response

*   **Success Response (200)**
    *   `SpawnerEntry` (SpawnerEntry) - The newly created SpawnerEntry object.

*   **Response Example**
    ```json
    {
      "snapshot": {
        "type": "ZOMBIE",
        "uuid": "..."
      },
      "spawnWeight": 10,
      "spawnRule": {
        "conditions": [],
        "actions": []
      },
      "equipment": {
        "itemInMainHand": {
          "type": "IRON_SWORD",
          "amount": 1
        }
      }
    }
    ```
```

### Get Short List from ConfigurationSection

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/configuration/MemorySection

Fetches a list of Short values based on the provided path. It returns a default or empty list if the path does not exist. Casting to Short is attempted, but incompatible values may not be included.

```java
/**
 * Gets the requested List of Short by path.
 * If the List does not exist but a default value has been specified, this will return the default value.
 * If the List does not exist and no default value was specified, this will return an empty List.
 * This method will attempt to cast any values into a Short if possible, but may miss any values out if they are not compatible.
 *
 * @param path Path of the List to get.
 * @return Requested List of Short.
 */
List<Short> getShortList(@NotNull String path);
```

### GET /configuration/list

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/configuration/MemorySection

Retrieves a List from the configuration based on the provided path. It handles cases where the List might not exist by returning a default value if specified, or null otherwise.

```APIDOC
## GET /configuration/list

### Description
Retrieves a List from the configuration based on the provided path. It handles cases where the List might not exist by returning a default value if specified, or null otherwise.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
/configuration/list

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **path** (string) - Required - The path of the List to retrieve.
- **def** (List) - Optional - The default List to return if the path is not found.

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **list** (List) - The requested List, or the default value if not found, or null if no default is provided and the List does not exist.

#### Response Example
{
  "list": [
    "example_item_1",
    "example_item_2"
  ]
}
```

### BooleanDialogInput Creation

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/io/papermc/paper/registry/data/dialog/input/class-use/BooleanDialogInput

Demonstrates how to create a new BooleanDialogInput instance using the DialogInput.bool() method.

```APIDOC
## POST /api/dialog/input/boolean

### Description
Creates a boolean dialog input with a specified key, label, initial value, and actions for true/false states.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/api/dialog/input/boolean

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **key** (String) - Required - A unique identifier for the dialog input.
- **label** (Component) - Required - The display label for the dialog input.
- **initial** (boolean) - Required - The initial state of the boolean input.
- **onTrue** (String) - Required - The action or key to use when the input is true.
- **onFalse** (String) - Required - The action or key to use when the input is false.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "key": "example_key",
  "label": {"text": "Enable Feature"},
  "initial": true,
  "onTrue": "enable_feature",
  "onFalse": "disable_feature"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **dialogInput** (BooleanDialogInput) - The created boolean dialog input object.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "dialogInput": {
    "type": "BOOLEAN",
    "key": "example_key",
    "label": {"text": "Enable Feature"},
    "initialValue": true,
    "valueIfTrue": "enable_feature",
    "valueIfFalse": "disable_feature"
  }
}
```
```

## POST /commands/help

POST

### Endpoint
`/commands/help`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
- **sender** (CommandSender) - Required - The source object which is executing this command.
- **currentAlias** (String) - Required - The alias of the command used.
- **args** (String[]) - Required - All arguments passed to the command, split via ' '.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "sender": "PlayerName",
  "currentAlias": "help",
  "args": ["plugin", "list"]
}
```

### Response

#### Response Example

{
  "success": true
}
```

## InlinedRegistryBuilderProvider.createInstrument(Consumer<RegistryBuilderFactory<MusicInstrument,? extends InstrumentRegistryEntry.Builder>> value)

Creates an inlined music instrument, allowing for immediate configuration using a builder.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/websites/jd_papermc_io_paper_1_21_11/api/instrument/create

    "factory": "io.papermc.paper.registry.data.InstrumentRegistryEntry.Builder",

      "duration": 3.0,
      "range": 15.0,

        "key": "minecraft:note.pling"
      }
    }
  }
}

### Response

#### Response Example

  "instrumentId": "custom_flute_123"
}
```

### TooltipDisplay.Builder Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/io/papermc/paper/datacomponent/item/TooltipDisplay.Builder

This section details the methods available on the TooltipDisplay.Builder interface for customizing tooltip display properties.

```APIDOC
## TooltipDisplay.Builder Methods

### Description
Provides methods to configure tooltip display properties, such as hiding the tooltip and adding or setting hidden components.

### Methods

#### `hideTooltip(boolean hide)`

*   **Description**: Controls whether the tooltip should be hidden.
*   **Method**: `TooltipDisplay.Builder`
*   **Parameters**:
    *   `hide` (boolean) - Required - `true` to hide the tooltip, `false` otherwise.

#### `addHiddenComponents(DataComponentType... components)`

*   **Description**: Adds one or more `DataComponentType` instances to be hidden.
*   **Method**: `TooltipDisplay.Builder`
*   **Parameters**:
    *   `components` (DataComponentType...) - Required - A variable number of component types to hide.

#### `hiddenComponents(Set<DataComponentType> components)`

*   **Description**: Sets the complete set of `DataComponentType` instances to be hidden, replacing any existing hidden components.
*   **Method**: `TooltipDisplay.Builder`
*   **Parameters**:
    *   `components` (Set<DataComponentType>) - Required - The set of component types to hide.

### Inherited Methods

#### `build()`

*   **Description**: Builds the `TooltipDisplay` component.
*   **Method**: Inherited from `io.papermc.paper.datacomponent.DataComponentBuilder`
```

### Bukkit API Package Overview

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/class-use/Translatable

This section provides a high-level overview of the main packages within the Bukkit API and their general purpose.

```APIDOC
## Bukkit API Packages

### Description
Provides a structured view of the Bukkit API, detailing key packages and their responsibilities.

### Packages

- **org.bukkit**: The root package, containing generalized API classes.
- **org.bukkit.attribute**: Classes related to entity attributes.
- **org.bukkit.block**: Classes for manipulating world voxels and their states.
- **org.bukkit.damage**: Classes for handling damage types and sources.
- **org.bukkit.enchantments**: Classes for item enchantments and metadata.
- **org.bukkit.entity**: Interfaces for non-voxel objects in the world.
- **org.bukkit.inventory**: Classes for managing player inventories and item interactions.
- **org.bukkit.inventory.meta.trim**: Classes for armor trim metadata.
- **org.bukkit.potion**: Classes for potion properties and effects.
```

### ConfigurationSection Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/configuration/MemorySection

This section details the various methods available on the ConfigurationSection interface, including retrieving path information, parent/root elements, and manipulating configuration values.

```APIDOC
## ConfigurationSection Interface

### Description
Provides methods for accessing and manipulating hierarchical configuration data.

### Methods

#### `getCurrentPath()`

##### Description
Retrieves the full path of this configuration section relative to its root.

##### Method
`getCurrentPath()`

##### Endpoint
N/A (Object method)

##### Returns
- `String` - The current path of the section.

#### `getName()`

##### Description
Gets the name of this individual `ConfigurationSection`, which is the final part of its path.

##### Method
`getName()`

##### Endpoint
N/A (Object method)

##### Returns
- `String` - The name of this section.

#### `getRoot()`

##### Description
Gets the root `Configuration` that contains this `ConfigurationSection`.

##### Method
`getRoot()`

##### Endpoint
N/A (Object method)

##### Returns
- `Configuration` or `null` - The root configuration, or null if the section is no longer contained within its root.

#### `getParent()`

##### Description
Gets the parent `ConfigurationSection` that directly contains this `ConfigurationSection`.

##### Method
`getParent()`

##### Endpoint
N/A (Object method)

##### Returns
- `ConfigurationSection` or `null` - The parent section, or null if this is a root configuration or no longer contained within its parent.

#### `addDefault(String path, Object value)`

##### Description
Sets the default value in the root at the given path. Creates a new `MemoryConfiguration` if no source is provided. Removes the value if it's null. Creates a new section if the value at the path is null.

##### Method
`addDefault(String path, Object value)`

##### Endpoint
N/A (Object method)

##### Parameters
- **path** (String) - Required - Path of the value to set.
- **value** (Object) - Optional - Value to set the default to.

#### `getDefaultSection()`

##### Description
Gets the equivalent `ConfigurationSection` from the default `Configuration` defined in the root.

##### Method
`getDefaultSection()`

##### Endpoint
N/A (Object method)

##### Returns
- `ConfigurationSection` or `null` - The equivalent section in the root configuration, or null if no defaults exist or the path does not contain a `ConfigurationSection`.

#### `set(String path, Object value)`

##### Method
`set(String path, Object value)`

##### Description
Sets the specified path to the given value. If the value is null, the entry is removed. Existing entries are replaced. Implementations may have limitations on storable types; `Configuration`s or `ConfigurationSection`s cannot be stored directly.

##### Endpoint
N/A (Object method)

##### Parameters
- **path** (String) - Required - Path of the object to set.
- **value** (Object) - Optional - New value to set the path to.

#### `get(String path)`

##### Method
`get(String path)`

##### Description
Gets the requested Object by path. Returns the default value if the object does not exist but a default is specified; otherwise, returns null.

##### Endpoint
N/A (Object method)

##### Parameters
- **path** (String) - Required - Path of the Object to get.

##### Returns
- `Object` or `null` - The requested Object or its default value, or null if not found.

#### `get(String path, Object def)`

##### Method
`get(String path, Object def)`

##### Description
Gets the requested Object by path, returning a default value if not found. If the Object does not exist, the specified default value is returned regardless of any root defaults.

##### Endpoint
N/A (Object method)

##### Parameters
- **path** (String) - Required - Path of the Object to get.
- **def** (Object) - Optional - Default value to return if the path is not found.

##### Returns
- `Object` - The requested Object or the provided default value.
```

@NotNull public @NotNull String getFullText(@NotNull @NotNull CommandSender sender)

Returns the full description of this help topic that is displayed when the user requests this topic's details. 
The result will be paginated to properly fit the user's client. 

Overrides:
    `getFullText` in class `HelpTopic`

    `sender` - The player or console requesting the full text. Useful for further security trimming the command's full text based on sub-permissions in custom implementations. 

Returns:
    A full topic description.

### io.papermc.paper.registry.keys.tags.PaintingVariantTagKeys.create

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/class-use/Art

Creates a tag key for Art in the minecraft:painting_variant registry.

```APIDOC
## PaintingVariantTagKeys.create(Key key)

### Description
Creates a tag key for `Art` in the `minecraft:painting_variant` registry, allowing for custom tags to be defined for painting variants.

### Method
`POST` (Implied - Creating a tag key)

### Endpoint
N/A (Static method)

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
- **key** (Key) - Required - The namespaced key for the tag.

### Request Example
```java
// Assuming 'myCustomTagKey' is a valid NamespacedKey
Key myCustomTagKey = NamespacedKey.minecraft("my_custom_tag");
TagKey<Art> customArtTag = PaintingVariantTagKeys.create(myCustomTagKey);
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **TagKey<Art>** - The created tag key for the Art.

#### Response Example
```json
// Represents the created tag key (not directly returned as JSON)
{
  "tag": "minecraft:my_custom_tag",
  "type": "Art"
}
```
```

### Entity Sound Retrieval - Java

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/entity/LivingEntity

Methods to get the specific sounds an entity makes during certain actions, such as death, eating, drinking, or falling. `getDeathSound()` gets the death sound, while `getDrinkingSound()` and `getEatingSound()` are item-specific.

```java
@Nullable Sound getDeathSound()
@NotNull Sound getDrinkingSound(@NotNull ItemStack itemStack)
@NotNull Sound getEatingSound(@NotNull ItemStack itemStack)

### Get Armor Contents from EntityEquipment (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/inventory/EntityEquipment

Retrieves all ItemStacks from the armor slots. Returns a copy for non-player entities or empty stacks, and a live mirror for non-empty stacks from players. The order is boots to helmet.

```java
EntityEquipment equipment = entity.getEquipment();
if (equipment instanceof PlayerInventory) {
    equipment.getArmorContents(); // will return an array of mirror
} else {
    equipment.getArmorContents(); // will return an array of copies
}
```

### Get Integer List from ConfigurationSection (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/configuration/MemorySection

Retrieves a List of Integers from the ConfigurationSection at the specified path. It attempts to cast values to Integers, returning an empty List if the key is not found or if no default is specified. Compatibility issues may lead to missed values.

```java
@NotNull public @NotNull List<Integer> getIntegerList(@NotNull @NotNull String path)
Description copied from interface: ConfigurationSection
Gets the requested List of Integer by path.
If the List does not exist but a default value has been specified, this will return the default value. If the List does not exist and no default value was specified, this will return an empty List.
This method will attempt to cast any values into an Integer if possible, but may miss any values out if they are not compatible.

Specified by:
    getIntegerList in interface ConfigurationSection

Parameters:
    path - Path of the List to get.

Returns:
    Requested List of Integer.
```

### Configuration and Metadata

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/overview-tree

Details classes related to configuration management and metadata handling within the server.

```APIDOC
## Configuration and Metadata

### Description
This section covers classes for handling server configurations and storing metadata associated with various game elements.

### Key Classes
*   **org.bukkit.configuration.MemorySection**: Implements org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection. Abstract base class for configuration data.
    *   **org.bukkit.configuration.MemoryConfiguration**: Implements org.bukkit.configuration.Configuration. Concrete implementation of configuration.
        *   **org.bukkit.configuration.file.FileConfiguration**: Abstract class for file-based configurations.
            *   **org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration**: Parses and saves YAML configuration files.
*   **org.bukkit.metadata.MetadataStoreBase<T>**: Base class for metadata storage.
    *   **org.bukkit.metadata.MetadataValueAdapter**: Implements org.bukkit.metadata.MetadataValue. Adapter for metadata values.
        *   **org.bukkit.metadata.LazyMetadataValue**: Handles lazily evaluated metadata.
            *   **org.bukkit.metadata.FixedMetadataValue**: Stores a fixed metadata value.

### Interfaces
*   org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection
*   org.bukkit.configuration.Configuration
*   org.bukkit.metadata.MetadataValue

### Deprecated Get Item in Hand (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/inventory/EntityEquipment

Deprecated method to get the item the entity is currently holding. Use `getItemInMainHand()` and `getItemInOffHand()` instead due to dual-wielding capabilities. Behavior regarding copies and mirrors is similar to `getItemInMainHand()`.

```java
@Deprecated(since="1.9") @NotNull ItemStack getItemInHand()
Deprecated.
entities can duel wield now use the methods for the specific hand instead
Gets the item the entity is currently holding 
This returns a copy if this equipment instance is from a non-player, or it's an empty stack (has AIR as its type). For non-empty stacks from players, this returns a live mirror. You can check if this will return a mirror with 
```

 EntityEquipment equipment = entity.getEquipment();
 if (equipment instanceof PlayerInventory) {
     equipment.getItemInHand(); // will return a mirror
 } else {
     equipment.getItemInHand(); // will return a copy
 }
 
```


Returns:
    the currently held item 

See Also:
    
      * `getItemInMainHand()`
      * `getItemInOffHand()`
```

### Registering Commands using BasicCommand in Bukkit Java Plugins

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/io/papermc/paper/command/brigadier/class-use/BasicCommand

This section outlines how `JavaPlugin` in `org.bukkit.plugin.java` allows for the registration of commands using the `BasicCommand` class.

```APIDOC
## POST /plugins/{pluginName}/commands/register (JavaPlugin)

### Description
Registers a command for a specific Java plugin using its label and a `BasicCommand` implementation.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/plugins/{pluginName}/commands/register`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **pluginName** (String) - Required - The name of the plugin.

#### Query Parameters
- **label** (String) - Required - The command label.
- **basicCommand** (BasicCommand) - Required - The `BasicCommand` implementation.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "label": "plugincommand",
  "basicCommand": "{ ... BasicCommand implementation ... }"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **void** - Indicates successful registration.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "status": "registered"
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## POST /plugins/{pluginName}/commands/register (JavaPlugin - With Description)

### Description
Registers a command for a Java plugin with a label, an optional description, and a `BasicCommand`.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/plugins/{pluginName}/commands/register`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **pluginName** (String) - Required - The name of the plugin.

#### Query Parameters
- **label** (String) - Required - The command label.
- **description** (String) - Optional - A description for the command.
- **basicCommand** (BasicCommand) - Required - The `BasicCommand` implementation.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "label": "plugdesc",
  "description": "Plugin command with description",
  "basicCommand": "{ ... BasicCommand implementation ... }"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **void** - Indicates successful registration.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "status": "registered"
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## POST /plugins/{pluginName}/commands/register (JavaPlugin - With Aliases)

### Description
Registers a command for a Java plugin with a label, a collection of aliases, and a `BasicCommand`.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/plugins/{pluginName}/commands/register`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **pluginName** (String) - Required - The name of the plugin.

#### Query Parameters
- **label** (String) - Required - The command label.
- **aliases** (Collection<String>) - Optional - A collection of aliases for the command.
- **basicCommand** (BasicCommand) - Required - The `BasicCommand` implementation.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "label": "pluginalias",
  "aliases": ["pa", "pcmd"],
  "basicCommand": "{ ... BasicCommand implementation ... }"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **void** - Indicates successful registration.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "status": "registered"
}
```
```

### AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent Details

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/event/entity/AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent

This section provides details on the AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent, including its constructors, methods, and inheritance hierarchy.

```APIDOC
## Class: AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent

### Description
Called when a lingering potion applies its effects. This event occurs every 5 ticks while the cloud is active.

### Inheritance
- `java.lang.Object`
  - `org.bukkit.event.Event`
    - `org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityEvent`
      - `org.bukkit.event.entity.AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent`

### Implements
- `Cancellable`

### Constructor Summary
- `AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent(@NotNull AreaEffectCloud entity, @NotNull List<LivingEntity> affectedEntities)`: Constructs an AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent.

### Method Summary
- `getAffectedEntities()`: Retrieves a mutable list of entities affected by the AreaEffectCloud.
- `getEntity()`: Returns the `AreaEffectCloud` entity involved in this event.
- `getHandlerList()`: Static method to get the handler list for this event.
- `getHandlers()`: Returns the handler list for this event.
- `isCancelled()`: Checks if the event has been cancelled.
- `setCancelled(boolean cancel)`: Sets the cancellation state of the event.

### Example Usage (Conceptual)
```java
// Subscribe to the event
Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvent(AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent.class, this, EventPriority.NORMAL, (listener, event) -> {
    AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent e = (AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent) event;
    AreaEffectCloud cloud = e.getEntity();
    List<LivingEntity> affected = e.getAffectedEntities();

    // Example: Log affected entities
    getLogger().info("AreaEffectCloud at " + cloud.getLocation() + " is applying effects to " + affected.size() + " entities.");

    // Example: Cancel the event if certain conditions are met
    // if (cloud.getDurationOnUse() < 10) {
    //     e.setCancelled(true);
    // }
}, this);
```

### Event Details
#### Constructor Details
##### AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent
`@Internal public AreaEffectCloudApplyEvent(@NotNull @NotNull AreaEffectCloud entity, @NotNull @NotNull List<LivingEntity> affectedEntities)`

#### Method Details
##### getEntity
`@NotNull public @NotNull AreaEffectCloud getEntity()`
Returns the Entity involved in this event.

Overrides: `getEntity` in class `EntityEvent`

Returns: Entity who is involved in this event.

##### getAffectedEntities
`@NotNull public @NotNull List<LivingEntity> getAffectedEntities()`
Retrieves a mutable list of the effected entities. It is important to note that not every entity in this list is guaranteed to be effected. The cloud may die during the application of its effects due to the depletion of `AreaEffectCloud.getDurationOnUse()` or `AreaEffectCloud.getRadiusOnUse()`.

Returns: the affected entity list.

##### isCancelled
`public boolean isCancelled()`
Gets the cancellation state of this event. A cancelled event will not be executed in the server, but will still pass to other plugins.

Specified by: `isCancelled` in interface `Cancellable`

Returns: `true` if this event is cancelled.

##### setCancelled
`public void setCancelled(boolean cancel)`
Sets the cancellation state of this event. A cancelled event will not be executed in the server, but will still pass to other plugins.

Specified by: `setCancelled` in interface `Cancellable`

Parameters:
- `cancel` - `true` if you wish to cancel this event.

##### getHandlers
`@NotNull public @NotNull HandlerList getHandlers()`

Specified by: `getHandlers` in class `Event`

##### getHandlerList
`@NotNull public static @NotNull HandlerList getHandlerList()`

```

Deprecated method to get a registry for a given class type. Use `RegistryAccess.getRegistry(io.papermc.paper.registry.RegistryKey)` instead. Returns null if no registry is found for the class. Not all registries present in `Registry` may be returned.

```java
/**
 * Deprecated. use `RegistryAccess.getRegistry(io.papermc.paper.registry.RegistryKey)` with keys from `RegistryKey`
 * Returns the registry for the given class.
 * If no registry is present for the given class null will be returned.
 * Depending on the implementation not every registry present in `Registry` will be returned by this method.
 *
 * @param tClass of the registry to get
 * @param <T> type of the registry
 * @return the corresponding registry or null if not present
 */
@Nullable @Deprecated(since="1.20.6") <T extends Keyed> @Nullable Registry<T> getRegistry(@NotNull @NotNull Class<T> tClass);

Initializes a JavaPlugin instance with a provided PluginLoader, server instance, plugin description, data folder, plugin file, and class loader. This method is part of the internal plugin initialization process.

```java
JavaPlugin javaPlugin = ...;
PluginLoader loader = ...;
Server server = ...;
PluginDescriptionFile description = ...;
File dataFolder = ...;
File file = ...;
ClassLoader classLoader = ...;
javaPlugin.init(loader, server, description, dataFolder, file, classLoader);
```

### AsyncPlayerPreLoginEvent Constructor

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/io/papermc/paper/connection/class-use/PlayerLoginConnection

Constructs a new AsyncPlayerPreLoginEvent with detailed player login information, including the PlayerLoginConnection.

```APIDOC
## POST /api/player/prelogin

### Description
This endpoint represents the creation of an asynchronous player pre-login event, which includes all necessary player and connection data.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/api/player/prelogin

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **name** (string) - Required - The name of the player.
- **ipAddress** (string) - Required - The IP address of the player.
- **rawAddress** (string) - Required - The raw IP address of the player.
- **uniqueId** (string) - Required - The unique identifier of the player.
- **transferred** (boolean) - Required - Indicates if the player is being transferred.
- **profile** (object) - Optional - The player profile object.
- **hostname** (string) - Required - The hostname of the connection.
- **playerLoginConnection** (object) - Required - The player login connection details.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "name": "PlayerName",
  "ipAddress": "192.168.1.100",
  "rawAddress": "192.168.1.100",
  "uniqueId": "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-1234-567890abcdef",
  "transferred": false,
  "profile": {},
  "hostname": "example.com",
  "playerLoginConnection": {
    "address": "192.168.1.100",
    "uniqueId": "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-1234-567890abcdef",
    "hostname": "example.com"
  }
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **eventStatus** (string) - The status of the pre-login event.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "eventStatus": "SUCCESS"
}
```
```

### Static Factory Methods for LoadingMap

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/co/aikar/util/LoadingMap

Provides static methods to create LoadingMap instances with various configurations, including custom loaders and auto-instantiation using reflection.

```APIDOC
## Static Factory Methods

### `of`

#### Description
Creates a new `LoadingMap` with the specified map and loader.

#### Method
`Map<K,V> of(Map<K,V> backingMap, Function<K,V> loader)`

#### Parameters
- `backingMap` (Map<K,V>) - Required - The actual map being used.
- `loader` (Function<K,V>) - Required - The loader to use for generating values.

#### Response Example
```java
// Example usage (conceptual)
Map<String, Integer> backingMap = new HashMap<>();
Function<String, Integer> loader = key -> key.length();
Map<String, Integer> autoMap = LoadingMap.of(backingMap, loader);
```

### `newAutoMap` (with keyClass)

#### Description
Creates a `LoadingMap` with an auto-instantiating loader. It will automatically construct the `Value` class when not found. This method uses reflection.

#### Method
`Map<K,V> newAutoMap(Map<K,V> backingMap, Class<? extends K> keyClass, Class<? extends V> valueClass)`

#### Parameters
- `backingMap` (Map<K,V>) - Required - The actual map being used.
- `keyClass` (Class<? extends K>) - Optional - The class used for the K generic.
- `valueClass` (Class<? extends V>) - Required - The class used for the V generic.

#### Response Example
```java
// Example usage (conceptual)
Map<String, Integer> backingMap = new HashMap<>();
Map<String, Integer> autoMap = LoadingMap.newAutoMap(backingMap, String.class, Integer.class);
```

### `newAutoMap` (without keyClass)

#### Description
Creates a `LoadingMap` with an auto-instantiating loader. It will automatically construct the `Value` class when not found. This method uses reflection.

#### Method
`Map<K,V> newAutoMap(Map<K,V> backingMap, Class<? extends V> valueClass)`

#### Parameters
- `backingMap` (Map<K,V>) - Required - The actual map being used.
- `valueClass` (Class<? extends V>) - Required - The class used for the V generic.

#### Response Example
```java
// Example usage (conceptual)
Map<String, Integer> backingMap = new HashMap<>();
Map<String, Integer> autoMap = LoadingMap.newAutoMap(backingMap, Integer.class);
```

### `newHashAutoMap` (with keyClass, no capacity/loadFactor)

#### Description
Creates an auto-initializing map using a `HashMap`.

#### Method
`Map<K,V> newHashAutoMap(Class<? extends K> keyClass, Class<? extends V> valueClass)`

#### Parameters
- `keyClass` (Class<? extends K>) - Optional - The class used for the K generic.
- `valueClass` (Class<? extends V>) - Required - The class used for the V generic.

#### Response Example
```java
// Example usage (conceptual)
Map<String, Integer> autoMap = LoadingMap.newHashAutoMap(String.class, Integer.class);
```

### `newHashAutoMap` (without keyClass, no capacity/loadFactor)

#### Description
Creates an auto-initializing map using a `HashMap`.

#### Method
`Map<K,V> newHashAutoMap(Class<? extends V> valueClass)`

#### Parameters
- `valueClass` (Class<? extends V>) - Required - The class used for the V generic.

#### Response Example
```java
// Example usage (conceptual)
Map<String, Integer> autoMap = LoadingMap.newHashAutoMap(Integer.class);
```

### `newHashAutoMap` (with keyClass, with capacity/loadFactor)

#### Description
Creates an auto-initializing map using a `HashMap` with specified initial capacity and load factor.

#### Method
`Map<K,V> newHashAutoMap(Class<? extends K> keyClass, Class<? extends V> valueClass, int initialCapacity, float loadFactor)`

#### Parameters
- `keyClass` (Class<? extends K>) - Optional - The class used for the K generic.
- `valueClass` (Class<? extends V>) - Required - The class used for the V generic.
- `initialCapacity` (int) - Required - Initial capacity to use.
- `loadFactor` (float) - Required - Load factor to use.

#### Response Example
```java
// Example usage (conceptual)
Map<String, Integer> autoMap = LoadingMap.newHashAutoMap(String.class, Integer.class, 16, 0.75f);
```

### `newHashAutoMap` (without keyClass, with capacity/loadFactor)

#### Description
Creates an auto-initializing map using a `HashMap` with specified initial capacity and load factor.

#### Method
`Map<K,V> newHashAutoMap(Class<? extends V> valueClass, int initialCapacity, float loadFactor)`

#### Parameters
- `valueClass` (Class<? extends V>) - Required - The class used for the V generic.
- `initialCapacity` (int) - Required - Initial capacity to use.
- `loadFactor` (float) - Required - Load factor to use.

#### Response Example
```java
// Example usage (conceptual)
Map<String, Integer> autoMap = LoadingMap.newHashAutoMap(Integer.class, 16, 0.75f);
```
```

### BrewingStartEvent API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/event/block/BrewingStartEvent

API documentation for the BrewingStartEvent class, which extends InventoryBlockStartEvent and is called when a brewing stand begins brewing.

```APIDOC
## BrewingStartEvent API

### Description
Called when a brewing stand starts to brew. This event allows modification of brewing times.

### Method
Event

### Endpoint
N/A (This is an event, not a REST endpoint)

### Parameters
This event does not have traditional API parameters as it's triggered by game events.

### Request Example
N/A

### Response
N/A

## Method Details

### `getBrewingTime()`

- **Description**: Gets the amount of brewing ticks left.
- **Return Type**: `int`
- **Annotations**: `@org.jetbrains.annotations.Range(from=0L, to=2147483647L)`

### `setBrewingTime(int brewTime)`

- **Description**: Sets the brewing ticks left.
- **Parameters**:
  - `brewTime` (int) - Required - The ticks left, which is no less than 0.
- **Throws**:
  - `IllegalArgumentException` - if the ticks are less than 0.

### `getRecipeBrewTime()`

- **Description**: Gets the recipe time for the brewing process which is used to compute the progress of the brewing process with `getBrewingTime()`.
- **Return Type**: `int`
- **Annotations**: `@Experimental`, `@org.jetbrains.annotations.Range(from=1L, to=2147483647L)`

### `setRecipeBrewTime(int recipeBrewTime)`

- **Description**: Sets the recipe time for the brewing process which is used to compute the progress of the brewing process with `getBrewingTime()`.
- **Parameters**:
  - `recipeBrewTime` (int) - Required - Recipe brew time (in ticks).
- **Throws**:
  - `IllegalArgumentException` - if the recipe brew time is non-positive.
- **Annotations**: `@Experimental`

### `getTotalBrewTime()` (Deprecated)

- **Description**: Deprecated, for removal: This API element is subject to removal in a future version. Use `getBrewingTime()` instead. Gets the total brew time associated with this event.
- **Return Type**: `int`
- **Annotations**: `@Deprecated(since="1.21", forRemoval=true)`

### `setTotalBrewTime(int brewTime)` (Deprecated)

- **Description**: Deprecated, for removal: This API element is subject to removal in a future version. Use `setBrewingTime(int)` instead. Sets the total brew time for this event.
- **Parameters**:
  - `brewTime` (int) - Required - The new total brew time.
- **Annotations**: `@Deprecated(since="1.21", forRemoval=true)`

```

}
```

### Methods:
- `columns(@org.checkerframework.checker.index.qual.Positive int columns)`: Sets the number of columns to display in the dialog.
- `exitAction(@Nullable ActionButton exitAction)`: Sets the action button to exit the dialog, or null if there is no exit action.
```

### Get and Set Spawner Required Player Range (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/block/TrialSpawner

Gets or sets the maximum squared distance a player can be for the spawner to remain active. A value of 0 or less makes the spawner always active if players are online. The default is 16.

```java
int getRequiredPlayerRange()
void setRequiredPlayerRange(int requiredPlayerRange)
```

Provides details about the AsyncPlayerConnectionConfigureEvent, including its purpose, constructors, and methods.

```APIDOC
## AsyncPlayerConnectionConfigureEvent

### Description
An event that allows you to configure the player connection asynchronously. This event occurs after configuration but before the player has entered the world. Once this event has finished execution, the player connection will continue.

### Method
Event

### Endpoint
N/A (Internal Bukkit Event)

### Parameters
N/A

### Request Example
N/A

### Response
#### Success Response (Event Execution)
- **PlayerConfigurationConnection** (PlayerConfigurationConnection) - The connection object that can be configured.

#### Response Example
```java
// Example of how to listen for and use the event
import io.papermc.paper.event.connection.configuration.AsyncPlayerConnectionConfigureEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;

public class PlayerConfigListener implements Listener {

    @EventHandler
    public void onAsyncPlayerConnectionConfigure(AsyncPlayerConnectionConfigureEvent event) {
        // Access and modify the connection configuration
        PlayerConfigurationConnection connection = event.getConnection();
        // ... perform configuration actions ...
    }
}
```

### Constructor Details
#### AsyncPlayerConnectionConfigureEvent
- **connection** (PlayerConfigurationConnection) - Required - The player's connection object.

### Method Details
#### getConnection
- **Returns**: PlayerConfigurationConnection - The current player connection.

#### getHandlers
- **Returns**: HandlerList - Returns the handler list for this event.

#### getHandlerList
- **Returns**: HandlerList - Returns the handler list for this event.
```

### PigZapEvent Details

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/event/entity/PigZapEvent

This section details the PigZapEvent, its constructors, and methods, including information on cancellation and entity transformations.

```APIDOC
## PigZapEvent

Stores data for pigs being zapped.

### Class Hierarchy
`java.lang.Object` -> `org.bukkit.event.Event` -> `org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityEvent` -> `org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityZapEvent` -> `com.destroystokyo.paper.event.entity.EntityZapEvent` -> `org.bukkit.event.entity.PigZapEvent`

### Implements
`Cancellable`

### Constructor Summary

*   **PigZapEvent**(`@NotNull Pig` pig, `@NotNull LightningStrike` bolt, `@NotNull PigZombie` zombifiedPiglin)
    *   Creates a new PigZapEvent.

### Method Summary

*   **getEntity**(): `@NotNull Pig`
    *   Returns the Entity involved in this event.
*   **getLightning**(): `@NotNull LightningStrike`
    *   Gets the bolt which is striking the pig.
*   **getPigZombie**(): `@NotNull PigZombie` (Deprecated)
    *   Deprecated. Use `EntityTransformEvent.getTransformedEntity()`.
    *   Gets the zombified piglin that will replace the pig, provided the event is not cancelled first.
*   **isCancelled**(): `boolean`
    *   Gets the cancellation state of this event. A cancelled event will not be executed in the server, but will still pass to other plugins.
*   **setCancelled**(boolean `cancel`): `void`
    *   Sets the cancellation state of this event. A cancelled event will not be executed in the server, but will still pass to other plugins.

### Constructor Details

*   ### PigZapEvent
    `@Internal public PigZapEvent(@NotNull @NotNull Pig pig, @NotNull @NotNull LightningStrike bolt, @NotNull @NotNull PigZombie zombifiedPiglin)`

### Method Details

*   ### getEntity
    `@NotNull public @NotNull Pig getEntity()`
    *   Description copied from class: `EntityEvent`
    *   Returns the Entity involved in this event.
    *   Overrides: `getEntity` in class `EntityEvent`
    *   Returns: Entity who is involved in this event

*   ### getLightning
    `@NotNull public @NotNull LightningStrike getLightning()`
    *   Gets the bolt which is striking the pig.
    *   Returns: lightning entity

*   ### getPigZombie
    `@NotNull @Deprecated(since="1.13.2") public @NotNull PigZombie getPigZombie()`
    *   Deprecated. Use `EntityTransformEvent.getTransformedEntity()`.
    *   Gets the zombified piglin that will replace the pig, provided the event is not cancelled first.
    *   Returns: resulting entity

*   ### isCancelled
    `public boolean isCancelled()`
    *   Description copied from interface: `Cancellable`
    *   Gets the cancellation state of this event. A cancelled event will not be executed in the server, but will still pass to other plugins.
    *   Specified by: `isCancelled` in interface `Cancellable`
    *   Overrides: `isCancelled` in class `EntityZapEvent`
    *   Returns: `true` if this event is cancelled

*   ### setCancelled
    `public void setCancelled(boolean cancel)`
    *   Description copied from interface: `Cancellable`
    *   Sets the cancellation state of this event. A cancelled event will not be executed in the server, but will still pass to other plugins.
    *   Specified by: `setCancelled` in interface `Cancellable`
    *   Overrides: `setCancelled` in class `EntityZapEvent`
    *   Parameters:
        *   `cancel` - `true` if you wish to cancel this event
```

### TypedKey Creation Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/io/papermc/paper/registry/class-use/TypedKey

Methods for creating TypedKey instances for different registry types.

```APIDOC
## Methods for Creating TypedKey

### Description
These methods allow for the creation of `TypedKey` instances for various Minecraft registry types.

### Method
`static TypedKey<T> create(Key key)`

### Endpoint
N/A (Java methods)

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
None

### Request Example
```java
// Example for BannerPattern
Key bannerPatternKey = Key.key("minecraft:flower");
TypedKey<PatternType> bannerPatternTypedKey = BannerPatternKeys.create(bannerPatternKey);

// Example for Biome
Key biomeKey = Key.key("minecraft:plains");
TypedKey<Biome> biomeTypedKey = BiomeKeys.create(biomeKey);

// Example for Cat Variant
Key catVariantKey = Key.key("minecraft:tabby");
TypedKey<Cat.Type> catVariantTypedKey = CatVariantKeys.create(catVariantKey);

// Example for Chicken Variant
Key chickenVariantKey = Key.key("minecraft:red");
TypedKey<Chicken.Variant> chickenVariantTypedKey = ChickenVariantKeys.create(chickenVariantKey);

// Example for Cow Variant
Key cowVariantKey = Key.key("minecraft:mooshroom");
TypedKey<Cow.Variant> cowVariantTypedKey = CowVariantKeys.create(cowVariantKey);

// Example for Damage Type
Key damageTypeKey = Key.key("minecraft:fall");
TypedKey<DamageType> damageTypeTypedKey = DamageTypeKeys.create(damageTypeKey);

// Example for Dialog
Key dialogKey = Key.key("minecraft:villager/ Nitwit");
TypedKey<Dialog> dialogTypedKey = DialogKeys.create(dialogKey);

// Example for Enchantment
Key enchantmentKey = Key.key("minecraft:sharpness");
TypedKey<Enchantment> enchantmentTypedKey = EnchantmentKeys.create(enchantmentKey);

// Example for Frog Variant
Key frogVariantKey = Key.key("minecraft:swamp");
TypedKey<Frog.Variant> frogVariantTypedKey = FrogVariantKeys.create(frogVariantKey);

// Example for Game Event
Key gameEventKey = Key.key("minecraft:block_destroy");
TypedKey<GameEvent> gameEventTypedKey = GameEventKeys.create(gameEventKey);

// Example for Music Instrument
Key instrumentKey = Key.key("minecraft:iron_xylophone");
TypedKey<MusicInstrument> instrumentTypedKey = InstrumentKeys.create(instrumentKey);

// Example for Jukebox Song
Key jukeboxSongKey = Key.key("minecraft:music_disc_13");
TypedKey<JukeboxSong> jukeboxSongTypedKey = JukeboxSongKeys.create(jukeboxSongKey);

// Example for Painting Variant
Key paintingVariantKey = Key.key("minecraft:bomb");
TypedKey<Art> paintingVariantTypedKey = PaintingVariantKeys.create(paintingVariantKey);

// Example for Pig Variant
Key pigVariantKey = Key.key("minecraft:squeal");
TypedKey<Pig.Variant> pigVariantTypedKey = PigVariantKeys.create(pigVariantKey);

// Example for Structure
Key structureKey = Key.key("minecraft:village");
TypedKey<Structure> structureTypedKey = StructureKeys.create(structureKey);

// Example for Trim Material
Key trimMaterialKey = Key.key("minecraft:iron");
TypedKey<TrimMaterial> trimMaterialTypedKey = TrimMaterialKeys.create(trimMaterialKey);

// Example for Trim Pattern
Key trimPatternKey = Key.key("minecraft:eye");
TypedKey<TrimPattern> trimPatternTypedKey = TrimPatternKeys.create(trimPatternKey);

// Example for Wolf Sound Variant
Key wolfSoundVariantKey = Key.key("minecraft:wolf_bark");
TypedKey<Wolf.SoundVariant> wolfSoundVariantTypedKey = WolfSoundVariantKeys.create(wolfSoundVariantKey);

// Example for Wolf Variant
Key wolfVariantKey = Key.key("minecraft:wolf_black");
TypedKey<Wolf.Variant> wolfVariantTypedKey = WolfVariantKeys.create(wolfVariantKey);

// Example for Zombie Nautilus Variant
Key zombieNautilusVariantKey = Key.key("minecraft:zombie_nautilus_normal");
TypedKey<ZombieNautilus.Variant> zombieNautilusVariantTypedKey = ZombieNautilusVariantKeys.create(zombieNautilusVariantKey);
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
N/A

#### Response Example
N/A
```

```APIDOC
## AbstractVillager API

### Description
The `AbstractVillager` interface represents a villager NPC in the PaperMC API. It extends several other interfaces to provide comprehensive functionality for managing villager-like entities.

### Methods

#### Instance Methods

- **`getInventory()`** (Inventory) - Gets this villager's inventory.
- **`resetOffers()`** (void) - Resets this villager's trade offers.

### Inherited Methods

- **`Frictional`**: `getFrictionState`, `setFrictionState`

- **`Merchant`**: (Inherits trading-related methods)
- **`Metadatable`**: (Methods for metadata management)
- **`Mob`**: (Methods related to mob behavior)
- **`Nameable`**: `getCustomName`, `setCustomName`
- **`NPC`**: (Methods for NPC behavior)
- **`Permissible`**: (Methods for permission checks)
- **`PersistentDataHolder`**: (Methods for persistent data)

### Get Component by Path and Decoder in Java

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/configuration/ConfigurationSection

Gets a formatted String as a Component by path, deserialized by the provided ComponentDecoder. Returns a fallback Component if the Component does not exist and a fallback is provided, otherwise returns null.

```java
@Nullable <C extends Component> C getComponent(@NotNull String path, @NotNull ComponentDecoder<? super String,C> decoder)
```

```java
@Contract("_, _, !null -> !null") @Nullable <C extends Component> C getComponent(@NotNull String path, @NotNull ComponentDecoder<? super String,C> decoder, @Nullable C fallback)
```

}

### World Particle Spawning (org.bukkit)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/class-use/Particle

Documentation for methods within the `World` class that allow spawning particles at specified locations with various configurations.

```APIDOC
## POST /bukkit/world/spawnParticle

### Description
Spawns particles in the world. Overloaded methods allow for different levels of control over particle appearance and target audience.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/bukkit/world/spawnParticle`

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **particle** (Particle) - Required - The type of particle to spawn.
- **location** (Location) - Required - The base location for the particles.
- **count** (int) - Required - The number of particles to spawn.
- **offsetX** (double) - Optional - The random offset on the X axis.
- **offsetY** (double) - Optional - The random offset on the Y axis.
- **offsetZ** (double) - Optional - The random offset on the Z axis.
- **extra** (double) - Optional - Additional data for certain particle types (e.g., size for `ITEM` particles).
- **data** (T) - Optional - Data for specific particle types (e.g., block state for `BLOCK` particles).
- **receivers** (List<Player>) - Optional - A list of players who should see the particle.
- **source** (Player) - Optional - The player that is considered the source of the particle.
- **force** (boolean) - Optional - Whether to force particle visibility even if clients have disabled them.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "particle": "FLAME",
  "location": {"x": 10.0, "y": 64.0, "z": 20.0},
  "count": 5,
  "offsetX": 0.1,
  "offsetY": 0.1,
  "offsetZ": 0.1,
  "extra": 0.5
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **status** (string) - "success" indicating the particles were spawned.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "status": "success"
}
```
```

### BoundingBox Constructors

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/util/BoundingBox

This section details the constructors available for creating BoundingBox objects.

```APIDOC
## BoundingBox Constructors

### BoundingBox()

Creates a new (degenerate) bounding box with all corner coordinates at `0`.

**Method:** Constructor

**Parameters:** None

### BoundingBox(double x1, double y1, double z1, double x2, double y2, double z2)

Creates a new bounding box from the given corner coordinates.

**Method:** Constructor

**Parameters:**
- **x1** (double) - Required - the first corner's x value
- **y1** (double) - Required - the first corner's y value
- **z1** (double) - Required - the first corner's z value
- **x2** (double) - Required - the second corner's x value
- **y2** (double) - Required - the second corner's y value
- **z2** (double) - Required - the second corner's z value
```

### BoundingBox Creation Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/util/class-use/BoundingBox

Methods for creating new BoundingBox objects from various inputs.

```APIDOC
## BoundingBox.of (Center and Extents)

### Description
Creates a new bounding box using the given center and extents.

### Method
`of(@NotNull Location center, double x, double y, double z)`

### Parameters
* **center** (`Location`) - Required - The center of the bounding box.
* **x** (`double`) - Required - The extent along the X-axis.
* **y** (`double`) - Required - The extent along the Y-axis.
* **z** (`double`) - Required - The extent along the Z-axis.

### Response Example
(Returns a new BoundingBox)

### Method
`of(@NotNull Vector center, double x, double y, double z)`

### Parameters
* **center** (`Vector`) - Required - The center of the bounding box.
* **x** (`double`) - Required - The extent along the X-axis.
* **y** (`double`) - Required - The extent along the Y-axis.
* **z** (`double`) - Required - The extent along the Z-axis.

### Response Example
(Returns a new BoundingBox)

## BoundingBox.of (Corners)

### Description
Creates a new bounding box using the coordinates of the given locations or vectors as corners.

### Method
`of(@NotNull Location corner1, @NotNull Location corner2)`

### Parameters
* **corner1** (`Location`) - Required - The first corner location.
* **corner2** (`Location`) - Required - The second corner location.

### Response Example
(Returns a new BoundingBox)

### Method
`of(@NotNull Vector corner1, @NotNull Vector corner2)`

### Parameters
* **corner1** (`Vector`) - Required - The first corner vector.
* **corner2** (`Vector`) - Required - The second corner vector.

### Response Example
(Returns a new BoundingBox)

## BoundingBox.of (Block)

### Description
Creates a new 1x1x1 sized bounding box containing the given block.

### Method
`of(@NotNull Block block)`

### Parameters
* **block** (`Block`) - Required - The block to create a bounding box for.

### Response Example
(Returns a new BoundingBox)

### Method
`of(@NotNull Block corner1, @NotNull Block corner2)`

### Parameters
* **corner1** (`Block`) - Required - The first corner block.
* **corner2** (`Block`) - Required - The second corner block.

### Response Example
(Returns a new BoundingBox)
```

## Execute Version Command

Executes the version command. This method is deprecated and will be removed in a future version. It is used internally to run the version command logic.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/execute/version

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
- **sender** (CommandSender) - Required - The source object which is executing this command.
- **currentAlias** (string) - Required - The alias of the command used.
- **args** (string[]) - Required - All arguments passed to the command, split via ' '.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "sender": "ConsoleSender",
  "currentAlias": "version",
  "args": []
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **success** (boolean) - True if the command was executed successfully, otherwise false.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "success": true
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## InventoryBlockStartEvent

### Description
This event is used when a block with an inventory starts an operation, such as a furnace smelting, a brewing stand brewing, or a campfire cooking.

## Constructor Summary

**Description**: Constructs a new InventoryBlockStartEvent.

*   **block** (`@NotNull Block`) - The block that is starting the operation.
*   **source** (`@NotNull ItemStack`) - The ItemStack that is initiating the operation.

## Method Summary

### `getSource()`

**Description**: Gets the source ItemStack for this event.

**Returns**: `@NotNull ItemStack` - the source ItemStack.

### `getHandlers()`

**Description**: Gets the handler list for this event.

**Returns**: `@NotNull HandlerList`

### `getHandlerList()`

### Plugin Loading API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/plugin/class-use/UnknownDependencyException

This section details the methods for loading plugins and the exceptions they may throw.

```APIDOC
## POST /api/plugins/load

### Description
Loads a plugin from the specified file. This method handles the process of discovering and initializing a plugin.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/api/plugins/load

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **file** (File) - Required - The file containing the plugin to be loaded.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "file": "path/to/your/plugin.jar"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **plugin** (Plugin) - The loaded plugin object.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "plugin": {
    "name": "MyPlugin",
    "version": "1.0.0"
  }
}
```

### Errors
- **UnknownDependencyException** - Thrown if the plugin has unmet dependencies.
- **InvalidPluginException** - Thrown if the plugin file is invalid or corrupted.
- **PluginException** - A general exception for other plugin loading errors.
```

### GameEventRegistryEntry.Builder

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/io/papermc/paper/registry/data/GameEventRegistryEntry.Builder

Provides methods to build and configure GameEventRegistryEntry instances.

```APIDOC
## GameEventRegistryEntry.Builder

### Description
A mutable builder for the `GameEventRegistryEntry`. Plugins may change in applicable registry events.

### Method
`range(int range)`

### Endpoint
N/A (This is a builder pattern, not a direct API endpoint)

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
None

### Request Example
```json
{
  "range": 5
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
This method returns the builder instance itself for method chaining.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "builderInstance": "GameEventRegistryEntry.Builder"
}
```

### Methods
#### `range`
Sets the range in which this game event should notify its listeners.

- **range** (int) - Required - The range of blocks. Must be between 0 and 2147483647.

Returns:
  `GameEventRegistryEntry.Builder` - This builder instance.

See Also:
  * `GameEventRegistryEntry.range()`
  * `GameEvent.getRange()`
```

### Plugin Loading and Registration

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/plugin/class-use/Plugin

APIs for loading plugins from a directory and registering events and services.

```APIDOC
## POST /api/plugins/load

### Description
Loads plugins from a specified directory. Note: This method is deprecated and subject to removal.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/api/plugins/load`

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **directory** (string) - Required - The directory containing plugin JAR files.
- **extraPluginJars** (array of strings) - Optional - A list of additional plugin JAR file paths.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "directory": "/path/to/plugins",
  "extraPluginJars": [
    "/path/to/extra1.jar",
    "/path/to/extra2.jar"
  ]
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - Confirmation of the loading process.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Plugins loaded successfully from specified directory."
}
```

## POST /api/plugins/register/event

### Description
Registers a custom event listener and executor for a specific event.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/api/plugins/register/event`

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **eventClass** (string) - Required - The fully qualified name of the event class.
- **listener** (object) - Required - The listener object.
- **priority** (string) - Required - The event priority (e.g., HIGHEST, HIGH, NORMAL, LOW, LOWEST).
- **executor** (object) - Required - The event executor.
- **plugin** (string) - Required - The name of the plugin registering the event.
- **ignoreCancelled** (boolean) - Optional - Whether to ignore cancelled events.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "eventClass": "org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerJoinEvent",
  "listener": {},
  "priority": "NORMAL",
  "executor": {},
  "plugin": "MyPlugin",
  "ignoreCancelled": false
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **status** (string) - Indicates the success of the event registration.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "status": "Event registered successfully."
}
```

## POST /api/plugins/register/events

### Description
Registers all events defined within a given listener class for a plugin.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/api/plugins/register/events`

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **listener** (object) - Required - The listener object containing event methods.
- **plugin** (string) - Required - The name of the plugin registering the events.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "listener": {},
  "plugin": "MyPlugin"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **status** (string) - Indicates the success of the event registration.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "status": "All events from listener registered successfully."
}
```
```

### Get Leggings from EntityEquipment (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/inventory/EntityEquipment

Retrieves the leggings currently worn by an entity. Returns a copy for non-player entities and a live mirror (or null) for player entities. The behavior can be checked by examining the EntityEquipment instance.

```java
EntityEquipment equipment = entity.getEquipment();
if (equipment instanceof PlayerInventory) {
    equipment.getLeggings(); // will return a mirror
} else {
    equipment.getLeggings(); // will return a copy
}
```

### Get Fixed Spawn Location (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/generator/ChunkGenerator

Gets a fixed spawn location for a given world. If a null value is returned, the world will attempt to find a spawn point randomly instead of using a fixed one. Parameters include the world and a random generator for calculations.

```java
@Nullable public @Nullable Location getFixedSpawnLocation(@NotNull @NotNull World world, @NotNull @NotNull Random random)
```

### Permissions API Overview

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/permissions/package-summary

This section provides an overview of the classes available within the org.bukkit.permissions package, detailing their purpose and relationships.

```APIDOC
## Bukkit Permissions Package Overview

### Description
This package is dedicated to providing binary state properties to players.

### Related Packages
* **org.bukkit**: The root package of the Bukkit API, containing generalized API classes.

### Classes and Interfaces

#### Interfaces
*   **Permissible**: Represents an object that may be assigned permissions.

#### Classes
*   **PermissibleBase**: Base `Permissible` for use in any `Permissible` object via proxy or extension.
*   **Permission**: Represents a unique permission that may be attached to a `Permissible`.
*   **PermissionAttachment**: Holds information about a permission attachment on a `Permissible` object.
*   **PermissionAttachmentInfo**: Holds information on a permission and which `PermissionAttachment` provides it.
*   **ServerOperator**: Represents an object that may become a server operator, such as a `Player`.

#### Enum Classes
*   **PermissionDefault**: Represents the possible default values for permissions.

#### Interfaces (Continued)
*   **PermissionRemovedExecutor**: Represents a class which is to be notified when a `PermissionAttachment` is removed from a `Permissible`.
```

--------------------------------

### Get ArmorStand LockType Enum Constants (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/paper/1.21.11/org/bukkit/entity/class-use/ArmorStand.LockType

Retrieve enum constants of ArmorStand.LockType. The `valueOf` method takes a String name to get a specific constant, while `values` returns all constants in declaration order. These are standard Java enum methods.

```java
ArmorStand.LockType armorStandLockType = ArmorStand.LockType.valueOf("SOME_LOCK_TYPE");
ArmorStand.LockType[] lockTypes = ArmorStand.LockType.values();
```
